## `AFG-4751-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AFG` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** Martindale-Abrasion Metrologist
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Retail Fabric Color Shading, Weaving Flaws & Length Metrology
